+                ? receivedPivotRow.data()
+                : localDist.data() +
+                  (pivot - sourceRows.first) * destinationColumns.count;

+                destinationColumns.count != 0) {

+                      pivotSourceOwner, grid.columnCommunicator);

+        }

+        const bool pivotColumnIsLocal = grid.columns == 1;
+        if (!pivotColumnIsLocal) {

+                const size_t localPivot = pivot - destinationColumns.first;
+                for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;

+                }

+                      static_cast<int>(sourceRows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                      pivotDestinationOwner, grid.rowCommunicator);

+            }

+                localDist.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;

+                localPath.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;

+                ? distanceRow[pivot - destinationColumns.first]

+                if (newDistance < distanceRow[localDestination]) {

+void unpackBlock(std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                 const unsigned int* const block,
+                 const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                 const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                 const size_t numNodes) {
+    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
+         ++localSource) {
+        for (size_t localDestination = 0;
+             localDestination < destinationColumns.count; ++localDestination) {
+            globalDist[idx2(sourceRows.first + localSource,
+                            destinationColumns.first + localDestination,
+                            numNodes)] =
+                block[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localDestination];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void collectDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
+                           std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                           const size_t numNodes,
+                           const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                           const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                           const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
+                           const int processCount) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalDist.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+        unpackBlock(globalDist, localDist.data(), sourceRows, destinationColumns,
+                    numNodes);
+
+        std::vector<unsigned int> receiveBuffer;
+        for (int process = 1; process < processCount; ++process) {
+            const int processRow = process / grid.columns;
+            const int processColumn = process % grid.columns;
+            const AxisPartition processSources =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processRow, grid.rows);
+            const AxisPartition processDestinations =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processColumn, grid.columns);
+            receiveBuffer.resize(processSources.count * processDestinations.count);
+            MPI_Recv(receiveBuffer.empty() ? nullptr : receiveBuffer.data(),
+                     static_cast<int>(receiveBuffer.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, process,
+                     COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackBlock(globalDist, receiveBuffer.data(), processSources,
+                        processDestinations, numNodes);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(localDist.empty() ? nullptr : localDist.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, 0,
+                 COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}

    61	int chooseProcessRows(const int processCount) noexcept {
    62	    for (int candidate = static_cast<int>(std::sqrt(processCount));
    63	         candidate >= 1; --candidate) {
    64	        if (processCount % candidate == 0) {
    65	            return candidate;
    66	        }
    67	    }
    68	    return 1;
    69	}

    71	struct ProcessGrid {
    72	    int rows;
    73	    int columns;
    74	    int row;
    75	    int column;
    76	    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator;
    77	    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator;
    78	};
    79	
    80	ProcessGrid makeProcessGrid(const int rank, const int processCount) {
    81	    const int gridRows = chooseProcessRows(processCount);
    82	    const int gridColumns = processCount / gridRows;
    83	    const int gridRow = rank / gridColumns;
    84	    const int gridColumn = rank % gridColumns;
    85	
    86	    ProcessGrid grid{gridRows, gridColumns, gridRow, gridColumn,
    87	                     MPI_COMM_NULL, MPI_COMM_NULL};
    88	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridRow, gridColumn, &grid.rowCommunicator);
    89	    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridColumn, gridRow,
    90	                   &grid.columnCommunicator);
    91	    return grid;
    92	}
    93	
    94	// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit seed through three instances of this LCG
    95	// per generated number.  Affine exponentiation lets every rank begin at its
    96	// exact segment of the original rand_r stream without root-side matrix setup.
    97	struct AffineTransform {
    98	    std::uint32_t multiplier;
    99	    std::uint32_t increment;
   100	};
   101	
   102	constexpr AffineTransform LCG_STEP{1103515245U, 12345U};
   103	
   104	AffineTransform compose(const AffineTransform outer,
   105	                        const AffineTransform inner) noexcept {
   106	    return {
   107	        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
   108	            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.multiplier),
   109	        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
   110	            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.increment +
   111	            outer.increment)};
   112	}
   113	
   114	std::uint32_t apply(const AffineTransform transform,
   115	                    const std::uint32_t state) noexcept {
   116	    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
   117	        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(transform.multiplier) * state +
   118	        transform.increment);
   119	}
   120	
   121	AffineTransform power(AffineTransform transform, size_t exponent) noexcept {
   122	    AffineTransform result{1U, 0U};
   123	    while (exponent != 0U) {
   124	        if ((exponent & 1U) != 0U) {
   125	            result = compose(transform, result);
   126	        }
   127	        transform = compose(transform, transform);
   128	        exponent >>= 1U;
   129	    }
   130	    return result;
   131	}
   132	
   133	AffineTransform randRAdvance(const size_t calls) noexcept {
   134	    const AffineTransform oneCall =
   135	        compose(LCG_STEP, compose(LCG_STEP, LCG_STEP));
   136	    return power(oneCall, calls);
   137	}
   138	
   139	bool canJumpRandRState() noexcept {
   140	    const AffineTransform oneCall = randRAdvance(1U);
   141	    std::uint32_t predictedState = 0x6d2b79f5U;
   142	    unsigned int actualState = predictedState;
   143	
   144	    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 4; ++iteration) {
   145	        (void)rand_r(&actualState);
   146	        predictedState = apply(oneCall, predictedState);
   147	        if (actualState != predictedState) {
   148	            return false;
   149	        }
   150	    }
   151	    return true;
   152	}

   154	void initializeDistanceMatrixFallback(
   155	    std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist, const size_t numNodes,
   156	    const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank, const int processCount) {
   157	    if (rank == 0) {

   168	                processSources.count * processDestinations.count);

   187	                    1U + static_cast<unsigned int>(range * rand_r(&seed) /
   188	                                                    static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
   189	                blocks[static_cast<size_t>(process)]
   190	                    [localSource * destinationPartition.count + localDestination] =
   191	                    source == destination ? 0U : value;
   192	            }
   193	        }
   194	
   195	        localDist = std::move(blocks[0]);
   196	        for (int process = 1; process < processCount; ++process) {
   197	            const std::vector<unsigned int>& block =
   198	                blocks[static_cast<size_t>(process)];
   199	            MPI_Send(block.empty() ? nullptr : block.data(),
   200	                     static_cast<int>(block.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, process,
   201	                     INITIALIZATION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   202	        }
   203	    } else {
   204	        MPI_Recv(localDist.empty() ? nullptr : localDist.data(),
   205	                 static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, 0,
   206	                 INITIALIZATION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   207	    }

   210	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
   211	                              const size_t numNodes,
   212	                              const AxisPartition sourceRows,
   213	                              const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
   214	                              const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
   215	                              const int processCount) {
   216	    int canJumpLocally = canJumpRandRState() ? 1 : 0;
   217	    int canJumpEverywhere = 0;
   218	    MPI_Allreduce(&canJumpLocally, &canJumpEverywhere, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN,
   219	                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   220	
   221	    if (canJumpEverywhere == 0) {
   222	        // This rare compatibility path retains exact rand_r results even on a
   223	        // C library whose state transition cannot be safely jumped ahead.
   224	        initializeDistanceMatrixFallback(localDist, numNodes, grid, rank,
   225	                                         processCount);
   226	        return;
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    const double range = static_cast<double>(MAX_DISTANCE);
   230	    const size_t firstStreamElement =
   231	        destinationColumns.first * numNodes + sourceRows.first;
   232	    unsigned int columnSeed = apply(randRAdvance(firstStreamElement), 42U);
   233	    const AffineTransform skipToNextDestination =
   234	        randRAdvance(numNodes - sourceRows.count);
   235	
   236	    for (size_t localDestination = 0;
   237	         localDestination < destinationColumns.count; ++localDestination) {
   238	        const size_t destination = destinationColumns.first + localDestination;
   239	        unsigned int seed = columnSeed;
   240	        for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
   241	             ++localSource) {
   242	            const size_t source = sourceRows.first + localSource;
   243	            const unsigned int value =
   244	                1U + static_cast<unsigned int>(range * rand_r(&seed) /
   245	                                                static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
   246	            localDist[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localDestination] =
   247	                source == destination ? 0U : value;
   248	        }
   249	        columnSeed = apply(skipToNextDestination, seed);
   250	    }
   251	}
   252	
   253	void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& localPath,
   254	                          const AxisPartition sourceRows,
   255	                          const AxisPartition destinationColumns) {
   256	    if (destinationColumns.count == 0) {
   257	        return;
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
   261	         ++localSource) {
   262	        unsigned int* const pathRow =
   263	            localPath.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;
   264	        for (size_t localDestination = 0;
   265	             localDestination < destinationColumns.count; ++localDestination) {
   266	            pathRow[localDestination] = static_cast<unsigned int>(
   267	                destinationColumns.first + localDestination);
   268	        }
   269	    }
   270	}
   271	
   272	void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
   273	                   std::vector<unsigned int>& localPath,
   274	                   const size_t numNodes,
   275	                   const AxisPartition sourceRows,
   276	                   const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
   277	                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
   278	    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(sourceRows.count);
   279	    std::vector<unsigned int> receivedPivotRow(destinationColumns.count);
   280	
   281	    for (size_t pivot = 0; pivot < numNodes; ++pivot) {
   282	        const int pivotSourceOwner = ownerOfIndex(pivot, numNodes, grid.rows);
   283	        const int pivotDestinationOwner =
   284	            ownerOfIndex(pivot, numNodes, grid.columns);
   285

   287	        if (grid.rows == 1) {
   288	            pivotValues = destinationColumns.count == 0
   289	                ? receivedPivotRow.data()
   290	                : localDist.data() +
   291	                  (pivot - sourceRows.first) * destinationColumns.count;
   292	        } else {
   293	            if (grid.row == pivotSourceOwner &&

   302	                      static_cast<int>(destinationColumns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   303	                      pivotSourceOwner, grid.columnCommunicator);
   304	            pivotValues = receivedPivotRow.data();
   305	        }
   306	
   307	        const bool pivotColumnIsLocal = grid.columns == 1;
   308	        if (!pivotColumnIsLocal) {
   309	            if (grid.column == pivotDestinationOwner) {
   310	                const size_t localPivot = pivot - destinationColumns.first;
   311	                for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
   312	                     ++localSource) {
   313	                    pivotColumn[localSource] =
   314	                        localDist[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localPivot];
   315	                }
   316	            }
   317	            MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.empty() ? nullptr : pivotColumn.data(),
   318	                      static_cast<int>(sourceRows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
   319	                      pivotDestinationOwner, grid.rowCommunicator);
   320	        }
   321	
   322	        if (destinationColumns.count == 0) {
   323	            continue;
   324	        }
   325	
   326	        const unsigned int* const __restrict pivotRow = pivotValues;
   327	        for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
   328	             ++localSource) {
   329	            if (sourceRows.first + localSource == pivot) {
   330	                continue;
   331	            }
   332

   334	                localDist.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;
   335	            unsigned int* const __restrict pathRow =
   336	                localPath.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;
   337	            const unsigned int distanceToPivot = pivotColumnIsLocal
   338	                ? distanceRow[pivot - destinationColumns.first]
   339	                : pivotColumn[localSource];

   481	        return 0;
   482	    }
   483	
   484	    const bool validSize =
   485	        numNodes <= static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) &&
   486	        (numNodes == 0 || numNodes <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / numNodes);
   487	    if (!validArguments || !validSize) {

   493	        MPI_Finalize();

   502	    const size_t localElements = sourceRows.count * destinationColumns.count;
   503

   506	        printf("Number of nodes: %zu\n", numNodes);
   507	        printf("MPI processes: %d (%d x %d grid)\n", processCount, grid.rows,

   517	    initializePathMatrix(localPath, sourceRows, destinationColumns);
   518	
   519	    if (rank == 0) {
   520	        printf("Computing shortest paths...\n");

   524	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

   527	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   528

   531	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   532

   534	        const long durationMilliseconds =
   535	            static_cast<long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   536	        const double operations = static_cast<double>(numNodes) * numNodes * numNodes;
   537	        const double gops = elapsedSeconds > 0.0
   538	                                ? operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9
   539	                                : std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();

   567	    }

   570	    MPI_Comm_free(&grid.rowCommunicator);

+constexpr int INITIALIZATION_TAG = 0;
+constexpr int COLLECTION_TAG = 1;
+
+static_assert(sizeof(unsigned int) == sizeof(std::uint32_t),
+              "This benchmark requires 32-bit unsigned int values");
+
+// This is the original column-major index used by result printing and
+// validation.  Distributed local blocks use row-major layout for their hot
+// update loop, where every destination in a local block is contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t source, const size_t destination,
+                             const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    return destination * numNodes + source;
+}
+
+struct AxisPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+AxisPartition partitionAxis(const size_t numNodes, const int partition,
+                            const int partitionCount) noexcept {
+    const size_t partitions = static_cast<size_t>(partitionCount);
+    const size_t base = numNodes / partitions;
+    const size_t extra = numNodes % partitions;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(partition);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, extra),
+            base + (index < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+int ownerOfIndex(const size_t index, const size_t numNodes,
+                 const int partitionCount) noexcept {
+    const size_t partitions = static_cast<size_t>(partitionCount);
+    const size_t base = numNodes / partitions;
+    const size_t extra = numNodes % partitions;
+    const size_t largerPartitions = (base + 1U) * extra;
+
+    if (index < largerPartitions) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(extra + (index - largerPartitions) / base);
+}
+
+int chooseProcessRows(const int processCount) noexcept {
+    for (int candidate = static_cast<int>(std::sqrt(processCount));
+         candidate >= 1; --candidate) {
+        if (processCount % candidate == 0) {
+            return candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    return 1;
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rows;
+    int columns;
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator;
+    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator;
+};
+
+ProcessGrid makeProcessGrid(const int rank, const int processCount) {
+    const int gridRows = chooseProcessRows(processCount);
+    const int gridColumns = processCount / gridRows;
+    const int gridRow = rank / gridColumns;
+    const int gridColumn = rank % gridColumns;
+
+    ProcessGrid grid{gridRows, gridColumns, gridRow, gridColumn,
+                     MPI_COMM_NULL, MPI_COMM_NULL};
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridRow, gridColumn, &grid.rowCommunicator);
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridColumn, gridRow,
+                   &grid.columnCommunicator);
+    return grid;
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit seed through three instances of this LCG
+// per generated number.  Affine exponentiation lets every rank begin at its
+// exact segment of the original rand_r stream without root-side matrix setup.
+struct AffineTransform {
+    std::uint32_t multiplier;
+    std::uint32_t increment;
+};
+
+constexpr AffineTransform LCG_STEP{1103515245U, 12345U};
+
+AffineTransform compose(const AffineTransform outer,
+                        const AffineTransform inner) noexcept {
+    return {
+        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.multiplier),
+        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.increment +
+            outer.increment)};
+}
+
+std::uint32_t apply(const AffineTransform transform,
+                    const std::uint32_t state) noexcept {
+    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(transform.multiplier) * state +
+        transform.increment);
+}
+
+AffineTransform power(AffineTransform transform, size_t exponent) noexcept {
+    AffineTransform result{1U, 0U};
+    while (exponent != 0U) {
+        if ((exponent & 1U) != 0U) {
+            result = compose(transform, result);
+        }
+        transform = compose(transform, transform);
+        exponent >>= 1U;
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+
+AffineTransform randRAdvance(const size_t calls) noexcept {
+    const AffineTransform oneCall =
+        compose(LCG_STEP, compose(LCG_STEP, LCG_STEP));
+    return power(oneCall, calls);
+}
+
+bool canJumpRandRState() noexcept {
+    const AffineTransform oneCall = randRAdvance(1U);
+    std::uint32_t predictedState = 0x6d2b79f5U;
+    unsigned int actualState = predictedState;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 4; ++iteration) {
+        (void)rand_r(&actualState);
+        predictedState = apply(oneCall, predictedState);
+        if (actualState != predictedState) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+

+                ownerOfIndex(destination, numNodes, grid.columns);

+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processColumn, grid.columns);
+            const size_t localDestination = destination - destinationPartition.first;

+void unpackBlock(std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                 const unsigned int* const block,
+                 const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                 const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                 const size_t numNodes) {
+    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
+         ++localSource) {
+        for (size_t localDestination = 0;
+             localDestination < destinationColumns.count; ++localDestination) {
+            globalDist[idx2(sourceRows.first + localSource,
+                            destinationColumns.first + localDestination,
+                            numNodes)] =
+                block[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localDestination];
+        }
+    }
+}

+void collectDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
+                           std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                           const size_t numNodes,
+                           const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                           const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                           const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
+                           const int processCount) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalDist.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+        unpackBlock(globalDist, localDist.data(), sourceRows, destinationColumns,
+                    numNodes);
+
+        std::vector<unsigned int> receiveBuffer;
+        for (int process = 1; process < processCount; ++process) {
+            const int processRow = process / grid.columns;
+            const int processColumn = process % grid.columns;
+            const AxisPartition processSources =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processRow, grid.rows);
+            const AxisPartition processDestinations =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processColumn, grid.columns);
+            receiveBuffer.resize(processSources.count * processDestinations.count);
+            MPI_Recv(receiveBuffer.empty() ? nullptr : receiveBuffer.data(),
+                     static_cast<int>(receiveBuffer.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, process,
+                     COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackBlock(globalDist, receiveBuffer.data(), processSources,
+                        processDestinations, numNodes);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(localDist.empty() ? nullptr : localDist.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, 0,
+                 COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
 constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
+constexpr int INITIALIZATION_TAG = 0;
+constexpr int COLLECTION_TAG = 1;
+
+static_assert(sizeof(unsigned int) == sizeof(std::uint32_t),
+              "This benchmark requires 32-bit unsigned int values");
+
+// This is the original column-major index used by result printing and
+// validation.  Distributed local blocks use row-major layout for their hot
+// update loop, where every destination in a local block is contiguous.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t source, const size_t destination,
+                             const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
+    return destination * numNodes + source;
+}
+
+struct AxisPartition {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+AxisPartition partitionAxis(const size_t numNodes, const int partition,
+                            const int partitionCount) noexcept {
+    const size_t partitions = static_cast<size_t>(partitionCount);
+    const size_t base = numNodes / partitions;
+    const size_t extra = numNodes % partitions;
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(partition);
+    return {index * base + std::min(index, extra),
+            base + (index < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
+}
+
+int ownerOfIndex(const size_t index, const size_t numNodes,
+                 const int partitionCount) noexcept {
+    const size_t partitions = static_cast<size_t>(partitionCount);
+    const size_t base = numNodes / partitions;
+    const size_t extra = numNodes % partitions;
+    const size_t largerPartitions = (base + 1U) * extra;
+
+    if (index < largerPartitions) {
+        return static_cast<int>(index / (base + 1U));
+    }
+    return static_cast<int>(extra + (index - largerPartitions) / base);
+}
+
+int chooseProcessRows(const int processCount) noexcept {
+    for (int candidate = static_cast<int>(std::sqrt(processCount));
+         candidate >= 1; --candidate) {
+        if (processCount % candidate == 0) {
+            return candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    return 1;
+}
+
+struct ProcessGrid {
+    int rows;
+    int columns;
+    int row;
+    int column;
+    MPI_Comm rowCommunicator;
+    MPI_Comm columnCommunicator;
+};
+
+ProcessGrid makeProcessGrid(const int rank, const int processCount) {
+    const int gridRows = chooseProcessRows(processCount);
+    const int gridColumns = processCount / gridRows;
+    const int gridRow = rank / gridColumns;
+    const int gridColumn = rank % gridColumns;
+
+    ProcessGrid grid{gridRows, gridColumns, gridRow, gridColumn,
+                     MPI_COMM_NULL, MPI_COMM_NULL};
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridRow, gridColumn, &grid.rowCommunicator);
+    MPI_Comm_split(MPI_COMM_WORLD, gridColumn, gridRow,
+                   &grid.columnCommunicator);
+    return grid;
+}
+
+// glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit seed through three instances of this LCG
+// per generated number.  Affine exponentiation lets every rank begin at its
+// exact segment of the original rand_r stream without root-side matrix setup.
+struct AffineTransform {
+    std::uint32_t multiplier;
+    std::uint32_t increment;
+};
+
+constexpr AffineTransform LCG_STEP{1103515245U, 12345U};
+
+AffineTransform compose(const AffineTransform outer,
+                        const AffineTransform inner) noexcept {
+    return {
+        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.multiplier),
+        static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(outer.multiplier) * inner.increment +
+            outer.increment)};
+}
+
+std::uint32_t apply(const AffineTransform transform,
+                    const std::uint32_t state) noexcept {
+    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(transform.multiplier) * state +
+        transform.increment);
+}
+
+AffineTransform power(AffineTransform transform, size_t exponent) noexcept {
+    AffineTransform result{1U, 0U};
+    while (exponent != 0U) {
+        if ((exponent & 1U) != 0U) {
+            result = compose(transform, result);
+        }
+        transform = compose(transform, transform);
+        exponent >>= 1U;
+    }
+    return result;
+}
+
+AffineTransform randRAdvance(const size_t calls) noexcept {
+    const AffineTransform oneCall =
+        compose(LCG_STEP, compose(LCG_STEP, LCG_STEP));
+    return power(oneCall, calls);
+}
+
+bool canJumpRandRState() noexcept {
+    const AffineTransform oneCall = randRAdvance(1U);
+    std::uint32_t predictedState = 0x6d2b79f5U;
+    unsigned int actualState = predictedState;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 4; ++iteration) {
+        (void)rand_r(&actualState);
+        predictedState = apply(oneCall, predictedState);
+        if (actualState != predictedState) {
+            return false;
+        }
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+

+void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
+                   std::vector<unsigned int>& localPath,
+                   const size_t numNodes,
+                   const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                   const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                   const ProcessGrid& grid) {
+    std::vector<unsigned int> pivotColumn(sourceRows.count);
+    std::vector<unsigned int> receivedPivotRow(destinationColumns.count);
+
+    for (size_t pivot = 0; pivot < numNodes; ++pivot) {
+        const int pivotSourceOwner = ownerOfIndex(pivot, numNodes, grid.rows);
+        const int pivotDestinationOwner =
+            ownerOfIndex(pivot, numNodes, grid.columns);
+
+        const unsigned int* pivotValues = nullptr;
+        if (grid.rows == 1) {
+            pivotValues = destinationColumns.count == 0
+                ? receivedPivotRow.data()
+                : localDist.data() +
+                  (pivot - sourceRows.first) * destinationColumns.count;
+        } else {
+            if (grid.row == pivotSourceOwner &&
+                destinationColumns.count != 0) {
+                const unsigned int* const localPivot =
+                    localDist.data() +
+                    (pivot - sourceRows.first) * destinationColumns.count;
+                std::copy_n(localPivot, destinationColumns.count,
+                            receivedPivotRow.data());
+            }
+            MPI_Bcast(receivedPivotRow.empty() ? nullptr : receivedPivotRow.data(),
+                      static_cast<int>(destinationColumns.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                      pivotSourceOwner, grid.columnCommunicator);
+            pivotValues = receivedPivotRow.data();
+        }
+
+        const bool pivotColumnIsLocal = grid.columns == 1;
+        if (!pivotColumnIsLocal) {
+            if (grid.column == pivotDestinationOwner) {
+                const size_t localPivot = pivot - destinationColumns.first;
+                for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
+                     ++localSource) {
+                    pivotColumn[localSource] =
+                        localDist[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localPivot];
+                }
+            }
+            MPI_Bcast(pivotColumn.empty() ? nullptr : pivotColumn.data(),
+                      static_cast<int>(sourceRows.count), MPI_UNSIGNED,
+                      pivotDestinationOwner, grid.rowCommunicator);
+        }
+
+        if (destinationColumns.count == 0) {
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        const unsigned int* const __restrict pivotRow = pivotValues;
+        for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
+             ++localSource) {
+            if (sourceRows.first + localSource == pivot) {
+                continue;
+            }
+
+            unsigned int* const __restrict distanceRow =
+                localDist.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;
+            unsigned int* const __restrict pathRow =
+                localPath.data() + localSource * destinationColumns.count;
+            const unsigned int distanceToPivot = pivotColumnIsLocal
+                ? distanceRow[pivot - destinationColumns.first]
+                : pivotColumn[localSource];
+
+            for (size_t localDestination = 0;
+                 localDestination < destinationColumns.count;
+                 ++localDestination) {
+                const unsigned int newDistance =
+                    distanceToPivot + pivotRow[localDestination];
+                if (newDistance < distanceRow[localDestination]) {
+                    distanceRow[localDestination] = newDistance;
+                    pathRow[localDestination] = static_cast<unsigned int>(pivot);
                 }
             }

+void unpackBlock(std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                 const unsigned int* const block,
+                 const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                 const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                 const size_t numNodes) {
+    for (size_t localSource = 0; localSource < sourceRows.count;
+         ++localSource) {
+        for (size_t localDestination = 0;
+             localDestination < destinationColumns.count; ++localDestination) {
+            globalDist[idx2(sourceRows.first + localSource,
+                            destinationColumns.first + localDestination,
+                            numNodes)] =
+                block[localSource * destinationColumns.count + localDestination];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void collectDistanceMatrix(const std::vector<unsigned int>& localDist,
+                           std::vector<unsigned int>& globalDist,
+                           const size_t numNodes,
+                           const AxisPartition sourceRows,
+                           const AxisPartition destinationColumns,
+                           const ProcessGrid& grid, const int rank,
+                           const int processCount) {
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        globalDist.resize(numNodes * numNodes);
+        unpackBlock(globalDist, localDist.data(), sourceRows, destinationColumns,
+                    numNodes);
+
+        std::vector<unsigned int> receiveBuffer;
+        for (int process = 1; process < processCount; ++process) {
+            const int processRow = process / grid.columns;
+            const int processColumn = process % grid.columns;
+            const AxisPartition processSources =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processRow, grid.rows);
+            const AxisPartition processDestinations =
+                partitionAxis(numNodes, processColumn, grid.columns);
+            receiveBuffer.resize(processSources.count * processDestinations.count);
+            MPI_Recv(receiveBuffer.empty() ? nullptr : receiveBuffer.data(),
+                     static_cast<int>(receiveBuffer.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, process,
+                     COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            unpackBlock(globalDist, receiveBuffer.data(), processSources,
+                        processDestinations, numNodes);
+        }
+    } else {
+        MPI_Send(localDist.empty() ? nullptr : localDist.data(),
+                 static_cast<int>(localDist.size()), MPI_UNSIGNED, 0,
+                 COLLECTION_TAG, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+}
